\u4f7f\u7528Kaiming\u521d\u59cb\u5316\n    43\t            for m in [self.fc1, self.fc2, self.fc3, self.out]:\n    44\t                torch.nn.init.kaiming_uniform_(m.weight, nonlinearity='leaky_relu')\n    45\t                torch.nn.init.zeros_(m.bias)\n    46\t\n    47\t        def forward(self, x):\n    48\t            x = x.type_as(self.fc1.weight)\n    49\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc1(x), 0.2)\n    50\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc2(x), 0.2)\n    51\t            x = F.leaky_relu(self.fc3(x), 0.2)\n    52\t            return self.out(x)\n    53\t\n    54\t\n    55\tclass DQNController(ParamController):\n    56\t    \"\"\"Simple continuous-action DQN controller for DE parameters.\n    57\t\n    58\t    The network outputs unbounded real values; `step` maps them to\n    59\t    DE parameter ranges: F in [0.1, 1.0], CR in [0.0, 1.0].\n    60\t    \"\"\"\n    61\t    def __init__(self, model_path: str = None, obs_dim: int = 3, device: str = 'cpu'):\n    62\t        if torch is None:\n    63\t            raise RuntimeError('torch is required for DQNController')\n    64\t        self.device = torch.device(device)\n    65\t        self.obs_dim = obs_dim\n    66\t        self.action_dim = 2  # F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>",

F, CR\n    67\t        self.net = _Net(self.obs_dim, self.action_dim).to(self.device)\n    68\t        if model_path and os.path.exists(model_path):\n    69\t            self.load(model_path)\n    70\t\n    71\t    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\n    72\t        return None\n    73\t\n    74\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n    75\t        it = float(observation.get('iteration', 0))\n    76\t        evals = float(observation.get('evals', 0))\n    77\t        gbest_f = float(observation.get('gbest_f', 0.0))\n    78\t        init_f = float(observation.get('initial_gbest_f', gbest_f))\n    79\t        max_evals = float(observation.get('max_evals', max(1.0, evals)))\n    80\t        # normalized progress and relative improvement\n    81\t        progress = min(1.0, evals / max(1.0, max_evals))\n    82\t        if init_f != 0.0 and gbest_f != 0.0 and np.isfinite(init_f) and np.isfinite(gbest_f):\n    83\t            rel_improve = max(0.0, min(1.0, (np.log(abs(init_f)) - np.log(abs(gbest_f))) / max(1.0, abs(np.log(abs(init_f))))))\n    84\t        else:\n    85\t            rel_improve = 0.0\n    86\t        obs = [progress, rel_improve, gbest_f]\n    87\t        \n    88\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n    89\t        x = torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device).unsqueeze(0)\n    90\t        \n    91\t        with torch.no_grad():\n    92\t            out_t = self.net(x).squeeze(0).cpu()\n    93\t            try:\n    94\t                out_t = torch.nan_to_num(out_t, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    95\t            except Exception:\n    96\t                out_np = out_t.numpy()\n    97\t                out_np = np.nan_to_num(out_np, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)\n    98\t                out_t = torch.from_numpy(out_np)\n    99\t            out_t = torch.clamp(out_t, -10.0, 10.0)\n   100\t\n   101\t        out = out_t.numpy()\n   102\t\n   103\t        # Map F (differential weight) with sigmoid to [0.1, 1.0]\n   104\t        try:\n   105\t            f_raw = float(out[0])\n   106\t            f_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-f_raw)))\n   107\t        except Exception:\n   108\t            f_sig = 0.5\n   109\t        F = 0.1 + (1.0 - 0.1) * f_sig\n   110\t        \n   111\t        # Map CR (crossover probability) with sigmoid to [0.0, 1.0]\n   112\t        try:\n   113\t            cr_raw = float(out[1])\n   114\t            cr_sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + float(np.exp(-cr_raw)))\n   115\t        except Exception:\n   116\t            cr_sig = 0.9\n   117\t        CR = 0.0 + (1.0 - 0.0) * cr_sig\n   118\t        \n   119\t        return {'F': float(F), 'CR': float(CR)}\n   120\t\n   121\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   122\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   123\t\n   124\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   125\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n   126\t\n   127\t\n   128\tclass DiscreteDQNController(ParamController):\n   129\t    \"\"\"Discrete-action DQN controller for DE parameters with online training.\n   130\t\n   131\t    Discretizes F and CR into `bins` equally spaced values and\n   132\t    treats the Cartesian product as the discrete action space.\n   133\t    The network outpu\n   134\t<response clipped>\n   135\trm]\n   136\t        net_dtype = self.net.fc1.weight.dtype\n   137\t        return torch.tensor(obs, dtype=net_dtype, device=self.device)\n   138\t\n   139\t    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\n   140\t        x = self._obs_to_tensor(observation)\n   141\t\n   142\t        # Epsilon-greedy action selection (only in training mode)\n   143\t        if self.training and self.rng.random() < self.epsilon:\n   144\t            idx = int(self.rng.randint(self.n_actions))\n   145\t        else:\n   146\t            with torch.no_grad():\n   147\t                q = self.net(x.unsqueeze(0)).squeeze(0).cpu().numpy()\n   148\t            idx = int(np.argmax(q))\n   149\t\n   150\t        if self.training:\n   151\t            # Store transition placeholder; reward will be set later via store_reward()\n   152\t            self._last_obs = observation\n   153\t            self._last_action_idx = idx\n   154\t            self._last_obs_tensor = x\n   155\t\n   156\t        return dict(self.actions[idx])\n   157\t\n   158\t    def store_reward(self, reward: float, next_observation: Dict[str, Any], done: bool):\n   159\t        \"\"\"Store a complete transition (obs, action, reward, next_obs, done) in the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   160\t        # Always track total reward (for both train and test)\n   161\t        self.total_reward += reward\n   162\t        self.current_sweep_reward += reward\n   163\t        \n   164\t        if not self.training:\n   165\t            return\n   166\t\n   167\t        next_obs_tensor = self._obs_to_tensor(next_observation)\n   168\t        transition = (\n   169\t            self._last_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   170\t            self._last_action_idx,\n   171\t            reward,\n   172\t            next_obs_tensor.clone(),\n   173\t            done\n   174\t        )\n   175\t\n   176\t        if len(self.replay_buffer) < self.buffer_capacity:\n   177\t            self.replay_buffer.append(transition)\n   178\t        else:\n   179\t            self.replay_buffer[self.buffer_pos] = transition\n   180\t            self.buffer_pos = (self.buffer_pos + 1) % self.buffer_capacity\n   181\t\n   182\t        self.step_count += 1\n   183\t\n   184\t        # Record return every N updates and reset counter\n   185\t        if self.step_count % self.return_record_interval == 0:\n   186\t            self.reward_history.append(self.current_sweep_reward)\n   187\t            self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   188\t\n   189\t        # Train every update_freq steps\n   190\t        if self.step_count % self.update_freq == 0 and len(self.replay_buffer) >= self.batch_size:\n   191\t            loss = self._train_step()\n   192\t            self.total_loss += loss\n   193\t            self.loss_history.append(loss)\n   194\t\n   195\t    def _train_step(self) -> float:\n   196\t        \"\"\"Perform one DQN training step on a batch from the replay buffer.\"\"\"\n   197\t        batch = self.rng.choice(len(self.replay_buffer), self.batch_size, replace=False).tolist()\n   198\t        batch = [self.replay_buffer[i] for i in batch]\n   199\t\n   200\t        obs_b = torch.stack([t[0] for t in batch])\n   201\t        act_b = torch.tensor([t[1] for t in batch], dtype=torch.long, device=self.device)\n   202\t        rew_b = torch.tensor([t[2] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   203\t        next_b = torch.stack([t[3] for t in batch])\n   204\t        done_b = torch.tensor([t[4] for t in batch], dtype=torch.float32, device=self.device)\n   205\t\n   206\t        q_values = self.net(obs_b)\n   207\t        q_sa = q_values.gather(1, act_b.unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)\n   208\t\n   209\t        with torch.no_grad():\n   210\t            q_next = self.target_net(next_b)\n   211\t            q_next_max = q_next.max(1)[0]\n   212\t            target = rew_b + self.gamma * (1.0 - done_b) * q_next_max\n   213\t\n   214\t        loss = F.mse_loss(q_sa, target)\n   215\t        self.optimizer.zero_grad()\n   216\t        loss.backward()\n   217\t        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(self.net.parameters(), self.max_grad_norm)\n   218\t        self.optimizer.step()\n   219\t\n   220\t        # Update target network periodically\n   221\t        if self.step_count % self.target_update_freq == 0:\n   222\t            self.target_net.load_state_dict(self.net.state_dict())\n   223\t            # Decay epsilon\n   224\t            self.epsilon = max(self.epsilon_end, self.epsilon * self.epsilon_decay)\n   225\t\n   226\t        return loss.item()\n   227\t\n   228\t    def reset_loss(self):\n   229\t        \"\"\"Reset loss and reward counters for a new sweep.\"\"\"\n   230\t        self.total_loss = 0.0\n   231\t        self.total_reward = 0.0\n   232\t        self.current_sweep_reward = 0.0\n   233\t        self.reward_history = []\n   234\t\n   235\t    def get_loss_history(self) -> list:\n   236\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded losses.\"\"\"\n   237\t        return self.loss_history\n   238\t\n   239\t    def get_reward_history(self) -> list:\n   240\t        \"\"\"Return the list of recorded returns (every N updates).\"\"\"\n   241\t        return self.reward_history\n   242\t\n   243\t    def get_total_loss(self) -> float:\n   244\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative training loss.\"\"\"\n   245\t        return self.total_loss\n   246\t\n   247\t    def get_total_reward(self) -> float:\n   248\t        \"\"\"Return the cumulative reward from environment interactions.\"\"\"\n   249\t        return self.total_reward\n   250\t\n   251\t    def action_to_index(self, action: Dict[str, float]) -> int:\n   252\t        a = np.array([action.get('F', 0.8), action.get('CR', 0.9)], dtype=float)\n   253\t        acts = np.array([[aa['F'], aa['CR']] for aa in self.actions], dtype=float)\n   254\t        d = np.sum((acts - a[None, :])**2, axis=1)\n   255\t        return int(np.argmin(d))\n   256\t\n   257\t    def save(self, path: str):\n   258\t        torch.save(self.net.state_dict(), path)\n   259\t\n   260\t    def load(self, path: str):\n   261\t        self.net.load_state_dict(torch.load(path, map_location=self.device))\n\"\n    }\n    </json>"

injected into `PSOOptimizer` to adjust parameters dynamically. This is\\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\\n\"\"\"\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\\n\\n# Import global configuration\\nimport sys\\nimport os\\ntry:\\n    sys.path.insert(0, \\'/hada\\')\\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\\n    # Set global seed at module load time\\n    set_global_seed()\\nexcept Exception:\\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\\n    SEED = 42\\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\\n        pass\\n\\n\\nclass ParamController(ABC):\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\\n\\n\\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\\n        self.params = dict(params)\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        return None\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        return dict(self.params)\\n\\n\\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\\n        self.w_start = w_start\\n        self.w_end = w_end\\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\\n        return {\\'w\\': w, \\'c1\\': c1, \\'c2\\': c2, \\'F\\': F, \\'CR\\': CR}\\n\\n\\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\\n\\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\\n      use \\'rand/1\\' strategy to explore broadly.\\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\\n      good building blocks), switch to \\'jade\\' for fast convergence.\\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get(\\'max_evals\\', 10000) / 100))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n\\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\\n\\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\\n        current_gbest = observation.get(\\'gbest_f\\', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\\n            strategy = \\'rand/1\\'\\n        else:\\n            strategy = \\'jade\\'\\n\\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\\n\\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get(\\'gbest_cv\\', None)\\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\\n\\n        return {\\'F\\': float(F), \\'CR\\': float(CR), \\'strategy\\': strategy,\\n                \\'epsilon\\': float(epsilon)}---\\n--- dqn_de_util ---\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_de_util.py\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_pso_util.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\"\\n    }\\n    </json>'\n/hada/outputs/bbob_unconstrained_eval/generate.log:780:2026-08-28 14:42:53,449 - injected into `PSOOptimizer` to adjust parameters dynamically. This is\\nthe hook that a DQN-based controller should implement.\\n\"\"\"\\nfrom abc import ABC, abstractmethod\\nfrom typing import Dict, Any\\n\\n# Import global configuration\\nimport sys\\nimport os\\ntry:\\n    sys.path.insert(0, \\'/hada\\')\\n    from config import set_global_seed, SEED\\n    # Set global seed at module load time\\n    set_global_seed()\\nexcept Exception:\\n    # Fallback if config module is not available\\n    SEED = 42\\n    def set_global_seed(*args, **kwargs):\\n        pass\\n\\n\\nclass ParamController(ABC):\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        \"\"\"Called at the start of optimization for a new problem/run.\"\"\"\\n\\n    @abstractmethod\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        \"\"\"Given an observation, return a dict of PSO parameters to use.\"\"\"\\n\\n\\nclass StaticController(ParamController):\\n    def __init__(self, params: Dict[str, float]):\\n        self.params = dict(params)\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        return None\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        return dict(self.params)\\n\\n\\nclass DummyHeuristicController(ParamController):\\n    \"\"\"A tiny heuristic that decays inertia weight and adjusts\\n    cognitive/social coefficients based on progress.\"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, w_start=0.9, w_end=0.4, decay_iters=100):\\n        self.w_start = w_start\\n        self.w_end = w_end\\n        self.decay_iters = decay_iters\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max(1, self.decay_iters))\\n        w = self.w_start * (1 - t) + self.w_end * t\\n        # Encourage exploration early, exploitation later\\n        c1 = 1.8 * (1.0 - 0.5 * t)\\n        c2 = 1.4 + 0.4 * t\\n        # Also support DE-style parameters for multi-objective DE optimizers\\n        F = 0.9 - 0.5 * t  # Large mutation early, refined late\\n        CR = 0.9 - 0.4 * t # More crossover early for diversity\\n        return {\\'w\\': w, \\'c1\\': c1, \\'c2\\': c2, \\'F\\': F, \\'CR\\': CR}\\n\\n\\nclass AdaptiveDEController(ParamController):\\n    \"\"\"Adaptive controller for DE/JADE algorithms with epsilon-constrained support.\\n\\n    - Early iterations: high F (exploration), high CR (diversity),\\n      use \\'rand/1\\' strategy to explore broadly.\\n    - Late iterations: lower F (exploitation), moderate CR (retain\\n      good building blocks), switch to \\'jade\\' for fast convergence.\\n    - Uses stagnation detection to temporarily boost F/CR.\\n    - Provides a decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained handling,\\n      allowing the optimizer to explore mildly infeasible regions early and\\n      enforcing feasibility as the budget is consumed.\\n    - If constraint violations are still large late in the run, epsilon is kept\\n      nonzero to avoid discarding useful search directions prematurely.\\n    \"\"\"\\n    def __init__(self, f_start=0.9, f_end=0.3, cr_start=0.9, cr_end=0.4,\\n                 strategy_change_iter=0.3, stagnation_boost=0.2,\\n                 epsilon_start=1e-2, epsilon_end=1e-8):\\n        self.f_start = f_start\\n        self.f_end = f_end\\n        self.cr_start = cr_start\\n        self.cr_end = cr_end\\n        self.strategy_change_iter = strategy_change_iter\\n        self.stagnation_boost = stagnation_boost\\n        self.epsilon_start = epsilon_start\\n        self.epsilon_end = epsilon_end\\n\\n    def reset(self, problem_meta: Dict[str, Any]) -> None:\\n        self.it = 0\\n        self.last_gbest = None\\n        self.last_gbest_cv = None\\n        self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n    def step(self, observation: Dict[str, Any]) -> Dict[str, float]:\\n        self.it = observation.get(\\'iteration\\', self.it + 1)\\n        max_iter = max(1, int(observation.get(\\'max_evals\\', 10000) / 100))\\n        t = min(1.0, float(self.it) / max_iter)\\n\\n        # Smooth progress-based decay from exploration to exploitation\\n        F = self.f_start * (1 - t) + self.f_end * t\\n        CR = self.cr_start * (1 - t) + self.cr_end * t\\n\\n        # Detect stagnation: no improvement in best fitness for several iterations\\n        current_gbest = observation.get(\\'gbest_f\\', None)\\n        if current_gbest is not None and self.last_gbest is not None:\\n            if current_gbest < self.last_gbest - 1e-12:\\n                self.stagnation_count = 0\\n            else:\\n                self.stagnation_count += 1\\n        self.last_gbest = current_gbest\\n\\n        # If stagnated for a while, add extra diversity\\n        if self.stagnation_count > 5:\\n            F = min(1.0, F + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            CR = min(1.0, CR + self.stagnation_boost)\\n            self.stagnation_count = 0\\n\\n        # Switch strategy: rand/1 early for exploration, jade later for convergence\\n        if t < self.strategy_change_iter:\\n            strategy = \\'rand/1\\'\\n        else:\\n            strategy = \\'jade\\'\\n\\n        # Decaying epsilon relaxation for epsilon-constrained DE.\\n        # Use log-scale interpolation for smooth transition.\\n        log_eps_start = float(np_log(self.epsilon_start))\\n        log_eps_end = float(np_log(self.epsilon_end))\\n        log_eps = log_eps_start + t * (log_eps_end - log_eps_start)\\n        epsilon = float(np_exp(log_eps))\\n\\n        # If the current best is still infeasible late in the run, relax epsilon\\n        # slightly so the algorithm is not forced into a tiny feasible region.\\n        current_gbest_cv = observation.get(\\'gbest_cv\\', None)\\n        if current_gbest_cv is not None and t > 0.5 and current_gbest_cv > epsilon:\\n            epsilon = max(epsilon, min(current_gbest_cv * 0.5, self.epsilon_start))\\n\\n        return {\\'F\\': float(F), \\'CR\\': float(CR), \\'strategy\\': strategy,\\n                \\'epsilon\\': float(epsilon)}---\\n--- dqn_de_util ---\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_de_util.py\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\\n/hada/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/dqn_pso_util.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/report.py\\n/hada/utils/domains/bbob_unconstrained/utils.py\"\\n    }\\n    </json>'\n/hada/outputs/bbob_unconstrained_eval/generate.log:780:2026-08-28 14:42:53,449 - Per-individual control parameters\\n                fi = float(np.clip(mu_F + 0.15 * rng.standard_cauchy(), 0.05, 1.2))\\n                cri = float(np.clip(mu_CR + 0.1 * rng.randn(), 0.0, 0.98))\\n\\n                #
